(herald
  "TLS 1.3 BASELINE-v2: server-only (tls13, tls13fs) and mutual (tls13-mtls, tls13mtlspfs) authentication")

(comment "CPSA 4.4.9")
(comment "All input read from tls13_baseline.scm")

(defprotocol tls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 4) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y)
        (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))
  (comment "1 in cohort - 1 not yet seen"))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 random32) (request response text) (spk akey)
    (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt) (y-0 rndx) (x-0 expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca)
    (y y-0) (x x-0))
  (precedes ((1 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y-0)
  (absent (y-0 x-0))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 4) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0)
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
                  sr-0)) (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0))
              (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
              sr-0))))))
  (label 1)
  (parent 0)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (p "server" z-0 (idx 4)) (p "server" "x" z-0 x)
      (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr)
      (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr) (cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca)
      (x x) (y y) (z 0)))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y)
        (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (y-0 (1 1)) (x (0 0))))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request text) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x expt)
    (y rndx))
  (defstrand server 4 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request) (spk spk) (s s)
    (ca ca) (y y) (x x))
  (non-orig (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x expt) (y rndx)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 4) (p "server" "cr" z cr)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "x" z x) (p "server" "y" z y) (non (privk ca))
          (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 1))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "client" z-0 4) (p "client" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "client" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "client" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "client" "s" z-0 s) (p "client" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "client" "y" z-0 y) (p "client" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (traces
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 2)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (p "client" z-0 (idx 4)) (p "client" "x" z-0 x)
      (p "client" "y" z-0 y) (p "client" "ca" z-0 ca)
      (p "client" "s" z-0 s) (p "client" "spk" z-0 spk)
      (p "client" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "client" "cr" z-0 cr) (cr cr) (sr sr)
      (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y) (z 0)))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y)
        (request request))))
  (origs (sr (0 1)))
  (ugens (y (0 1))))

        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (deflistener
    (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
        (x rndx) (y expt) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "request" z request)
          (p "client" "response" z response) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv
       (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
         (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
      (send
        (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
  (label 3)
  (unrealized (0 1) (1 0))
  (preskeleton)
  (maps)
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))
  (comment "Not a skeleton"))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)

(defprotocol tls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defprotocol tls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defprotocol tls13fs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies

          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))

  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) self
              (invk spk) (enc (hash self (invk spk)) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv request) (send request))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt (pv self spk (invk spk))))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-0 "nil")) (send spk))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-1 ignore))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt (pv self spk (invk spk)))))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt (pv self spk (invk spk))))
      (recv (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc
            (cat self (invk spk)
              (enc (hash self (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
                  sr-0)) (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0))
              (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) self (invk spk)
              (enc (hash self (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
              sr-0))))))
  (label 18)
  (parent 17)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (fact false) (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
      (response response) (spk spk) (ca ca) (s self) (self self)
      (priv-stor priv-stor) (x x) (y y) (z 0) (z-0 1) (z-1 2)))
  (maps
    ((0 1 2)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (request request) (response response) (spk spk)
        (ca ca) (s self) (self self) (priv-stor priv-stor) (x x)
        (y y))))

  (ugens (spk (3 1)) (y-0 (4 2)) (x (0 0))))

(defprotocol tls13fs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

(defprotocol tls13-mtls basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx

(defprotocol tls13-mtls basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg

(defprotocol tls13-mtls basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defprotocol tls13-mtls basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton tls13-mtls
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 cr-1 sr-1 random32) (request response text)
    (spk cpk spk-0 akey) (s ca c s-0 name) (x expt) (y x-0 rndx)
    (y-0 expt) (y-1 rndx) (x-1 expt))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c) (y y)
    (x x))

    (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
  (defstrand client 3 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (spk spk-0) (cpk cpk) (s s-0)
    (ca ca) (c c) (x x-0) (y y-0))

    (y y-1) (x x-1))

  (non-orig (invk cpk) (privk ca))

      ((cr sr random32) (request text) (spk cpk akey) (s ca c name)

          (p "server" "s" z s) (p "server" "c" z c)

(defprotocol tls13-mtls basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton tls13-mtls
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 cr-1 sr-1 random32) (request response text)
    (spk cpk spk-0 akey) (s ca c s-0 name) (x expt) (y x-0 rndx)
    (y-0 expt) (y-1 rndx) (x-1 expt))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c) (y y)
    (x x))
  (deflistener
    (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
  (defstrand client 3 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (spk spk-0) (cpk cpk) (s s-0)
    (ca ca) (c c) (x x-0) (y y-0))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-1) (sr sr-1) (spk spk-0) (s s-0) (ca ca)
    (y y-1) (x x-1))
  (precedes ((0 1) (1 0)) ((2 2) (0 2)) ((3 1) (2 1)))
  (non-orig (invk cpk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y x-0 y-1)
  (absent (y x) (y-1 x-1))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (request text) (spk cpk akey) (s ca c name)
        (x expt) (y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "server" "cr" z cr)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (p "server" "request" z request)
          (p "server" "spk" z spk) (p "server" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "server" "s" z s) (p "server" "c" z c)
          (p "server" "ca" z ca) (p "server" "y" z y)
          (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))) (non (invk cpk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 1)) (false))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca)) (2 1))
  (strand-map 0 1 2)
  (traces
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv
       (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
         (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
      (send
        (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
    ((send (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc (cat s-0 spk-0 (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-0 y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
                  sr-0)) (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0))
              (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s-0 spk-0
              (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-0 y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0 sr-0))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-0 y-0)) "c hs traffic" cr-0 sr-0))
            (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)) (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
            (cat s-0 spk-0 (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-0 y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
                  sr-0)) (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0))
              (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s-0 spk-0
              (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca))) c cpk
            (enc (hash "client" c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-0 y-0)) "c hs traffic" cr-0 sr-0))))
    ((recv (cat cr-1 (exp (gen) x-1)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr-1 (exp (gen) y-1))
          (enc (cat s-0 spk-0 (enc (hash s-0 spk-0) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-1 x-1)) "s hs traffic" cr-1
                  sr-1)) (cat cr-1 (exp (gen) x-1))
              (cat sr-1 (exp (gen) y-1)) s-0 spk-0

              sr-1))))))

  (ugens (y-1 (3 1)) (x-0 (2 0)) (y (0 1))))

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (ca s c self name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pval) (priv-stor cpriv-stor locn)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 6 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c)
    (cpriv-stor cpriv-stor) (x x) (y y))
  (deflistener request)
  (defstrand privkey-disclose 3 (pk spk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (precedes ((0 3) (2 0)))
  (non-orig (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr request)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
        (ca s c self name) (priv-stor locn) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 z-1 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (p "client" "sr" z sr)
          (p "client" "request" z request)
          (p "client" "response" z response) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "cpk" z cpk) (p "client" "s" z s)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y)
          (p "" "x" z-0 request) (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z-1 spk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z-1 self)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "priv-stor" z-1 priv-stor)
          (prec z 3 z-1 0) (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 1)
          (uniq-at request z 4)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((load cpriv-stor (cat pt (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))

        (priv-stor priv-stor) (x x) (y y) (cpriv-stor cpriv-stor))))
  (origs (pt-0 (3 1)) (pt (4 1)) (pt-1 (2 1)) (request (0 4))
    (cr (0 1)))
  (ugens (spk (3 1)) (y-0 (5 2)) (cpk (4 1)) (x (0 1))))

(comment "Nothing left to do")

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
              (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))
